```python
"""
Minimize score = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2 for nonnegative a.

The search treats the objective on the simplex sum(a)=1.  It combines:
- LP-based Frank-Wolfe-like directions: maximize sum(g) s.t. a*g <= current peak.
- Projected smoothed-subgradient (Euclidean) polishing.
- Multiplicative/mirror descent steps for robustness.
- Multiple lengths and diverse initializations.
- A final selection using the provided evaluate_sequence() when available.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


# ---------- Basic utilities ----------

def project_simplex(v):
    """Project vector v onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=1}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    mask = u * idx > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not mask.any():
        rho = 0
    else:
        rho = int(np.where(mask)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / (rho + 1.0)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = out.sum()
    if s > 0.0:
        out /= s
    else:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return out


def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    """Clip to nonnegative finite values and normalize to sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr.astype(np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    arr /= s
    return arr


def full_conv(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a, using FFT for larger n."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 256:
        return np.asarray(np.convolve(a, a, mode="full"), dtype=np.float64)
    size = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:2 * n - 1]
    return np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)


def score_of(a):
    """Internal score for normalized a: 2*n*max(conv(a,a))."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return math.inf
    b = full_conv(arr)
    if b.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def softmax_weights(b, tau):
    """Softmax over convolution coefficients b with temperature tau."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if tau <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(tau):
        tau = 1e-12
    z = b / tau
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(b.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        return w
    w = e / s
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    s2 = float(w.sum())
    if s2 > 0.0:
        w /= s2
    return w


def conv_gradient(a, lam):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k lam[k] * (a*a)[k].
    lam has length 2n-1; gradient has length n.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    lam = np.asarray(lam, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        val = 0.0
        if lam.size >= 1:
            val = 2.0 * float(lam[0]) * float(a[0])
        return np.array([val], dtype=np.float64)

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    # g[i] = sum_j a[j] * lam[i+j]
    for j in range(n):
        aj = float(a[j])
        if aj != 0.0:
            g += aj * lam[j:j + n]
    return 2.0 * g


# ---------- Local update methods ----------

def mix_line_search(a, g, current_score):
    """Line search over convex mixtures (1-t)a + t g."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    g = normalize_nonnegative(g)
    best_cand = None
    best_sc = float(current_score)

    for t in (0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 1.0, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01):
        cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
        cand = normalize_nonnegative(cand)
        sc = score_of(cand)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_cand = cand

    if best_cand is not None and best_sc < float(current_score) - 1e-12:
        return best_cand, best_sc
    return None, best_sc


def lp_direction(a, T):
    """
    LP direction: maximize sum(g) subject to g>=0 and a*g <= T.
    Returns normalized g, or None on failure.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if linprog is None or n <= 0 or n > 600:
        return None

    K = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        A[i:i + n, i] = a

    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.full(K, float(T), dtype=np.float64)

    res = None
    try:
        res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, None), method="highs",
                      options={"time_limit": 3.0})
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, None), method="highs")
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None:
        return None
    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return None

    status = getattr(res, "status", 0)
    # Infeasible or failed without a usable primal.
    if status in (2, 4, 5):
        return None

    g = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    g /= s
    return g


def euler_step(a, b, vel, eta, beta):
    """Projected smoothed-subgradient step with line search."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return None, score_of(a), vel

    m = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    cur = 2.0 * n * m
    tau = max(1e-18, 0.05 * m)
    lam = softmax_weights(b, tau)
    grad = conv_gradient(a, lam)

    vel = beta * vel + (1.0 - beta) * grad
    vnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(vel))
    if vnorm <= 1e-15:
        return None, cur, vel

    base = eta / max(1.0, vnorm)
    best_cand = None
    best_sc = cur

    for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 2.0, 0.05):
        step = base * factor
        cand = project_simplex(a - step * vel)
        sc = score_of(cand)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_cand = cand

    if best_cand is not None and best_sc < cur - 1e-12:
        return best_cand, best_sc, vel
    return None, best_sc, vel


def mirror_step(a, b, eta):
    """Multiplicative weights / mirror-descent step with line search."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return None, score_of(a)

    m = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    cur = 2.0 * n * m
    tau = max(1e-18, 0.05 * m)
    lam = softmax_weights(b, tau)
    grad = conv_gradient(a, lam)
    if grad.size != n:
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    a_safe = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    best_cand = None
    best_sc = cur

    shift_base = grad - float(np.min(grad))
    for factor in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta, 2.0 * eta):
        shift = -factor * shift_base
        shift = np.clip(shift, -50.0, 0.0)
        cand = a_safe * np.exp(shift)
        cand = normalize_nonnegative(cand)
        sc = score_of(cand)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_cand = cand

    if best_cand is not None and best_sc < cur - 1e-12:
        return best_cand, best_sc
    return None, best_sc


def maybe_symmetrize(a):
    """Try reflection symmetry; keep it only if it lowers score."""
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    sym = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1].copy())
    sym = normalize_nonnegative(sym)
    sc0 = score_of(a)
    sc1 = score_of(sym)
    if sc1 + 1e-12 < sc0:
        return sym, sc1
    return a, sc0


# ---------- Optimizer for one start ----------

def optimize_one_start(a_init, n, time_budget, rng, use_lp=True):
    """Run LP + gradient polishing for one initial simplex point."""
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a_init)
    if a.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(n, a.size)
        tmp[:m] = a[:m]
        a = normalize_nonnegative(tmp)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0), math.inf
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), 2.0

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_of(a)
    vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    eta = 0.35
    beta = 0.5
    eta_mirror = 2.0
    do_lp = bool(use_lp)
    lp_fail = 0

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    lp_limit = t0 + (0.65 * total if use_lp else 0.0)

    # Phase 1: LP-driven moves, with fallback local steps if LP stalls.
    while do_lp and time.monotonic() < min(lp_limit, t0 + total):
        a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
        b = full_conv(a)
        M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        cur = 2.0 * n * M
        if cur < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur
            best = a.copy()
        if M <= 0.0:
            break

        g = lp_direction(a, M)
        improved = False

        if g is not None:
            lp_fail = 0
            cand, sc = mix_line_search(a, g, cur)
            if cand is not None and sc < cur - 1e-12:
                a = cand
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best = a.copy()
                improved = True
        else:
            lp_fail += 1
            if lp_fail >= 8:
                do_lp = False

        if not improved:
            cand, sc, vel = euler_step(a, b, vel, eta, beta)
            if cand is not None and sc < cur - 1e-12:
                a = cand
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best = a.copy()
                lp_fail = 0
                continue

            cand_m, sc_m = mirror_step(a, b, eta_mirror)
            if cand_m is not None and sc_m < cur - 1e-12:
                a = cand_m
                if sc_m < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc_m
                    best = a.copy()
                lp_fail = 0
                continue

            # Perturb from best solution and reheat.
            a = normalize_nonnegative(best + 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n))
            vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            eta = 0.2 + 0.3 * rng.random()
            eta_mirror = 0.8 + 1.5 * rng.random()
            beta = 0.3 + 0.4 * rng.random()
            lp_fail = 0

    # Phase 2: local gradient/mirror polishing until budget expires.
    a = best.copy()
    vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    eta = 0.25
    eta_mirror = 1.5
    stall = 0
    polish_start = time.monotonic()
    polish_budget = max(0.0, t0 + total - polish_start)

    while polish_budget > 0.0 and time.monotonic() - polish_start < polish_budget:
        a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
        b = full_conv(a)
        M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        cur = 2.0 * n * M
        if cur < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur
            best = a.copy()
            stall = 0

        cand, sc, vel = euler_step(a, b, vel, eta, beta)
        if cand is not None and sc < cur - 1e-12:
            a = cand
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = a.copy()
            stall = 0
            eta = min(1.0, eta * 1.02)
            continue

        cand_m, sc_m = mirror_step(a, b, eta_mirror)
        if cand_m is not None and sc_m < cur - 1e-12:
            a = cand_m
            if sc_m < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_m
                best = a.copy()
            stall = 0
            continue

        stall += 1
        if stall > 8:
            a = normalize_nonnegative(best + 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n))
            vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            eta = 0.1 + 0.3 * rng.random()
            eta_mirror = 0.5 + 1.5 * rng.random()
            beta = 0.3 + 0.4 * rng.random()
            stall = 0
        else:
            eta *= 0.85
            eta_mirror *= 0.9
            if eta < 1e-4 and eta_mirror < 1e-2:
                break

    a_final, sc_final = maybe_symmetrize(best)
    if sc_final + 1e-12 < best_sc:
        best = a_final
        best_sc = sc_final

    return best, best_sc


# ---------- Initializations ----------

def init_uniform(n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)


def init_u_shape(n):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    d = np.minimum(i, float(n - 1) - i) + 1.0
    a = 1.0 / np.sqrt(d)
    a += 1e-6
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def init_clusters(n, rng, centers, sigma_frac):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    span = max(1, n - 1)
    for c in centers:
        mu = c * span
        sig = max(1.0, sigma_frac * span)
        a += np.exp(-((i - mu) / sig) ** 2)
    a += 1e-5 + 0.01 * np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def init_cosine(n, rng):
    freq = int(2 + rng.integers(0, 3))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    denom = max(1, n - 1)
    a = (1.0 + freq * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x / float(denom))) ** 2
    a += 1e-6 + 0.01 * np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def init_random(n, rng):
    a = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.1
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def resample_pattern(pattern, n):
    """Interpolate a one-dimensional pattern to length n and smooth slightly."""
    p = np.asarray(pattern, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p /= s

    if p.size == 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if p.size == n:
        return p.copy()

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    vals = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)

    ksize = min(5, n)
    if ksize > 1 and n > 1:
        kernel = np.ones(ksize, dtype=np.float64) / float(ksize)
        vals = np.convolve(vals, kernel, mode="same")

    return normalize_nonnegative(vals)


def get_inits(n, rng, base_arr):
    inits = []

    if base_arr is not None and base_arr.size > 0:
        base_arr = np.asarray(base_arr, dtype=np.float64)
        if base_arr.size == n:
            inits.append(normalize_nonnegative(base_arr))
        else:
            inits.append(resample_pattern(base_arr, n))

    inits.append(init_uniform(n))
    inits.append(init_u_shape(n))
    inits.append(init_clusters(n, rng, [0.22, 0.78], 0.10))
    inits.append(init_clusters(n, rng, [0.10, 0.40, 0.60, 0.90], 0.08))
    inits.append(init_cosine(n, rng))
    inits.append(init_random(n, rng))

    # For larger n, reduce variety to keep search time reasonable.
    if n > 128:
        inits = inits[:4]

    return inits


# ---------- Final evaluation helper ----------

def clean_sequence_list(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return []
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in arr]


def safe_eval(seq):
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        if np.isfinite(val):
            return val
    except Exception:
        pass
    return math.inf


# ---------- Main proposed candidate function ----------
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)

    # Read previous construction if available.
    base_arr = None
    try:
        base_arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if base_arr.ndim != 1:
            base_arr = None
    except Exception:
        base_arr = None

    pool = []
    base_pool = []

    # Base candidates are evaluated later to avoid slowing down the main search.
    if base_arr is not None and base_arr.size > 0:
        b = np.where(np.isfinite(base_arr), base_arr, 0.0)
        b = np.clip(b, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if b.sum() > 0.0:
            if b.size <= 10000:
                base_pool.append(("base_clip", b, score_of(b)))
                bn = normalize_nonnegative(b)
                base_pool.append(("base_norm", bn, score_of(bn)))

    # Conservative fallback.
    pool.append(("uniform128", init_uniform(128), score_of(init_uniform(128))))

    best_global_a = pool[0][1]
    best_global_sc = pool[0][2]

    # Planned lengths.  Length 128 is primary; larger lengths are tried if time remains.
    n_plan = []
    if 128 not in [n for n, _ in n_plan]:
        n_plan.append((128, 2.0))

    base_n = int(base_arr.size) if base_arr is not None else 0
    if 16 <= base_n <= 512 and base_n != 128:
        n_plan.append((base_n, 1.0))
    if 256 not in [n for n, _ in n_plan]:
        n_plan.append((256, 0.8))
    if 512 not in [n for n, _ in n_plan]:
        n_plan.append((512, 0.4))

    remaining_weight = sum(w for _, w in n_plan)
    print(f"[plan] n_plan={[(n, round(w, 3)) for n, w in n_plan]}")

    for idx_plan, (n, w) in enumerate(n_plan):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 20.0:
            break

        time_left = max(0.0, deadline - now)
        time_n = (w / max(1e-12, remaining_weight)) * time_left * 0.92
        time_n = max(20.0, min(1600.0, time_n))
        remaining_weight -= w

        if time_n < 15.0 or time_left < 25.0:
            continue

        inits = get_inits(n, rng, base_arr)
        per_init = time_n / max(1, len(inits))
        best_n_a = None
        best_n_sc = math.inf

        print(f"[search] n={n}, allocated={time_n:.1f}s, inits={len(inits)}")

        for ii, a_init in enumerate(inits):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= deadline - 10.0:
                break
            tb = min(per_init, deadline - now - 5.0)
            tb = max(8.0, min(260.0, tb))

            use_lp = (linprog is not None and n <= 512)
            a_best, sc_best = optimize_one_start(a_init, n, tb, rng, use_lp=use_lp)

            if a_best is None or a_best.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(a_best)):
                a_best = init_uniform(n)
                sc_best = score_of(a_best)

            pool.append((f"n{n}_init{ii}", a_best, float(sc_best)))

            if sc_best < best_n_sc:
                best_n_sc = sc_best
                best_n_a = a_best.copy()
            if sc_best < best_global_sc:
                best_global_sc = sc_best
                best_global_a = a_best.copy()

            print(f"[search] n={n} init={ii} score={sc_best:.6f} t_left={deadline - time.monotonic():.1f}")

        # Extra polish of the best result for this length.
        now = time.monotonic()
        extra = min(0.35 * time_n, deadline - now - 10.0)
        if best_n_a is not None and extra > 12.0:
            use_lp = (linprog is not None and n <= 512)
            a_pol, sc_pol = optimize_one_start(best_n_a, n, extra, rng, use_lp=use_lp)
            if a_pol is not None and a_pol.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(a_pol)):
                pool.append((f"n{n}_polish", a_pol, float(sc_pol)))
                if sc_pol < best_n_sc:
                    best_n_sc = sc_pol
                    best_n_a = a_pol.copy()
                if sc_pol < best_global_sc:
                    best_global_sc = sc_pol
                    best_global_a = a_pol.copy()
                print(f"[polish] n={n} score={sc_pol:.6f}")

    pool.extend(base_pool)

    # Final selection: prefer actual evaluator values; internal score as backup.
    best_seq_list = None
    best_val = math.inf
    best_int = math.inf

    eval_deadline = time.monotonic() + 20.0
    for name, arr, internal in pool:
        if time.monotonic() > eval_deadline:
            break
        intr = float(internal) if internal is not None and np.isfinite(internal) else math.inf
        seq = clean_sequence_list(arr)
        if not seq or sum(seq) < 0.01:
            continue
        val = safe_eval(seq)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_seq_list = seq
            best_int = intr
        if intr < best_int and (best_seq_list is None or val == math.inf):
            best_int = intr
            best_seq_list = seq

    if best_seq_list is None or len(best_seq_list) == 0 or sum(best_seq_list) < 0.01:
        best_seq_list = clean_sequence_list(init_uniform(128))
        best_val = safe_eval(best_seq_list)

    print(f"[final] eval={best_val:.6f} internal={best_int:.6f} n={len(best_seq_list)}")
    return best_seq_list
```
